summary = "Evidence-Inference Reconstruction（EIR）通过结构化状态积累来源证据，并在最后一次模型调用中重建推理，以更少调用提升多跳问答准确率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33778"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "EIR 将证据检索与最终答案推理分离，以结构化状态引导单条检索轨迹并持续积累来源证据。即使中间推理出错并混入无关证据，只要相关证据已被召回，模型仍可通过一次最终调用重建正确推理。在 HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA 和 MuSiQue 各自匹配的 1,000 道题上，EIR 的 Answer F1 相比基线提升 8.3～32.8 个百分点，并优于 Agentic SSR 和 Reflexion。EIR 平均每题仅需 4.85 次模型调用，显著低于 Agentic SSR 的 35.29 次和 Reflexion 的 12.41 次。"
purpose = "- 为多跳智能体提供一种比反复重试推理轨迹更低成本的错误恢复方案。\n- 表明检索阶段的关键目标应是保证相关证据覆盖，而非要求每个中间推理步骤均完全正确。\n- 可用于优化深度研究、检索增强问答等系统的准确率、调用次数与推理延迟。\n- 启示智能体将结构化证据状态与最终答案生成解耦，以提高系统对中间错误和噪声证据的容忍度。"
notes = "标题：Evidence-Inference Reconstruction: When The Evidence Is Recalled But The Reasoning Goes Wrong\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33778\n摘要：Modern multi-hop LLM agents are equipped with built-in mechanisms to detect errors in intermediate reasoning steps. Such errors trigger corrective actions from these agents, which mostly follow the paradigm of retrying the steps or the reasoning trajectories. Not only are these retries expensive, we present in this paper that they are also potentially unnecessary. To this end, we introduce Evidence-Inference Reconstruction (EIR), which uses structured state to guide one retrieval trajectory, accumulating source evidence in the process. We show that as long as the relevant evidence has been collected, EIR is capable of generating the correct answer in a single final model call even if erroneous evidence has been mixed in due to incorrect intermediate reasoning steps. In one evaluation, usin"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33728"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "ALDER将世界模型表示为显式、透明且可检验的参数化方程，并利用数值优化器拟合方程系数。独立验证器在留出数据上检验候选规律，成本与安全感知的选择器则主动发起干预实验，以区分多个同样符合已有轨迹的假设。新实验产生的反例被写入证据账本，用于驱动后续结构修正和淘汰不相容规律。实验表明，该方法能够发现初始公式集合之外的规律、修复失败模型、减少假设判别所需交互，并提升分布外预测及机器人目标控制能力。"
purpose = "- 为可解释世界模型提供“提出方程—拟合参数—独立验证—主动干预—结构修正”的闭环范式。\n- 说明主动实验可突破固定轨迹和预定义候选空间的限制，更高效地区分竞争性因果或动力学假设。\n- 可用于自动化科学发现、机器人动力学辨识，以及需要安全和成本约束的现实世界实验设计。\n- 经过验证的显式规律可直接用于逆问题求解，使模型发现与目标导向控制自然衔接。"
notes = "标题：ALDER: Discovering the Laws of a World by Acting in It\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33728\n摘要：Reliable world models should not only predict future states but express how actions change the world in an explicit, transparent and testable form, such as equations. Yet methods that rely on a fixed set of trajectories cannot distinguish equally good competing hypotheses, while searches over a fixed set of predefined candidates cannot discover equations outside the initial hypothesis space. We introduce ALDER (Action-guided Law Discovery, Evaluation, and Revision), a method that actively proposes novel experiments to test and revise models. Specifically, ALDER proposes parametric equations; a numerical optimizer fits their coefficients; an independent verifier tests these candidates on held-out data. To distinguish between competing valid hypotheses, a cost- and safety-aware selector quer"


[[items]]
id = "cognit-guard"
title = "COGNIT-Guard：面向低延迟与低误报的置信度级联安全护栏"
subtopic = "置信度评估"
topics = [
    "人工智能安全治理",
    "不确定性估计",
    "模型路由",
    "成本优化",
    "系统优化",
]
research = []
keywords = [
    "置信度评估",
    "模型路由",
    "不确定性估计",
    "安全评测",
    "系统优化",
]
orgs = [
    "Guangdong Polytechnic Normal University",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "COGNIT-Guard通过校准后的CPU快速门控与NPU直判模型置信度级联，在显式延迟SLO下兼顾安全检测准确率、概率校准和低良性误报率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33671"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "COGNIT-Guard是一种用于目标大模型预填充前安全筛查的独立直判式护栏，无需生成文本或读取目标模型隐藏状态。系统先由经过验证集校准的CPU门控器快速判断，仅将低置信度样本升级至NPU上的322M参数双向模型Laya-322M，并采用非对称误报惩罚控制双用途请求的误拦截。在DUCS-Bench未见测试集上，该方法达到98.85%准确率、0.42%良性误报率、1.12% ECE和0.0104 Brier分数；部署级CPU-NPU串行级联平均延迟为41.63毫秒，并取得99.23%准确率与0%误报率。SafetyBench-ZH实验显示领域训练会带来一定OOD对齐损失，而经验回放可将OOD准确率恢复至64.10%—65.05%，同时保持接近100%的域内准确率。"
purpose = "- 为实时大模型安全网关提供一种无需解码的低延迟直判方案，适合在目标模型处理请求之前执行预筛查。\n- 展示概率校准与置信度升级机制可用于协调CPU和NPU算力，在明确延迟SLO下动态分配推理成本。\n- 非对称误报惩罚有助于减少合规咨询、双用途安全研究等良性请求被错误拦截的问题。\n- 经验回放可缓解安全模型领域适配造成的OOD能力退化，为护栏持续更新提供训练策略参考。"
notes = "标题：COGNIT-Guard: Calibrated Standalone Direct-Decision Guardrails with Heterogeneous CPU-NPU Confidence Cascading under Explicit Latency and False-Positive Constraints\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33671\n摘要：When must a foundation-model safety gateway generate tokens, and when should it directly output a calibrated decision? We study calibrated standalone direct-decision foundation models for real-time pre-ingestion safety guardrails, jointly addressing probability calibration, dual-use false-positive control, and heterogeneous CPU-NPU routing under explicit latency SLOs. Pre-ingestion guardrails must screen prompts prior to target-LLM prefill with low false alarms on benign compliance inquiries; however, shallow classifiers are brittle to phrasing shifts, hidden-state probes require coupling to a target LLM, and generative guards incur high decoding latency and dual-use false positives. We present COGNIT-Guard, coupling a validation-calibrated CPU fast gatekeeper with confidence-gated escalat"


[[items]]
id = "item-110"
title = "训练期间模型行为的可扩展归因与控制"
subtopic = "训练归因"
topics = [
    "数据归因",
    "模型训练",
    "安全对齐",

]
research = []
keywords = [
    "训练归因",
    "数据归因",
    "模型训练",
    "样本重加权",
    "过程监督",
]
orgs = [
    "Rice University",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "论文提出基于行为梯度唯一性（BGU）的 BS-Ghost 算法，以较低训练开销实时归因单个样本对模型行为的影响，并通过调整样本权重控制后续学习方向。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33667"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文用互信息刻画批次内样本干扰，衡量整体行为变化能够揭示多少单个样本贡献信息，并证明该互信息是行为梯度唯一性（BGU）的对数函数。BS-Ghost 通过在批次空间共享计算来估计归因分数，无需存储与模型同等规模的逐样本梯度。针对完整的 1,000 样本 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 训练任务，该方法仅增加 27 秒、约 8.0% 的额外开销。移除样本并重新训练的实验表明，BGU 能识别因果性塑造最终行为的数据；带符号信息还可判断样本强化或削弱目标行为的方向。系统可进一步预测调整样本权重对下一次更新的影响，并据此将模型行为引导至期望目标。"
purpose = "- 为训练数据归因提供可嵌入训练循环的低开销方法，便于定位真正塑造模型行为的样本。\n- 支持持续监测目标行为在训练过程中的形成机制，增强训练流程的可审计性与可解释性。\n- 可依据带符号的样本贡献动态调整训练权重，为模型对齐和行为干预提供直接控制手段。\n- BGU 与 BS-Ghost 可作为训练管线监督、数据质量分析及异常样本排查的基础组件。"
notes = "标题：Scalable Attribution and Control of Model Behavior During Training\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33667\n摘要：Attributing and controlling model behavior during training requires identifying each example's contribution quickly enough to act before the next update. However, examples in the same training batch can produce similar behavioral changes, making their individual contributions difficult to distinguish. We address this ambiguity through mutual information, accounting for interference within the batch by quantifying how much the combined behavioral change reveals about each example's contribution. We show that this mutual information is a logarithmic function of Behavioral Gradient Uniqueness (BGU). BGU gives the information measure its geometric interpretation. Our Batch-Space Ghost (BS-Ghost) algorithm makes these scores practical inside the training loop through shared computation in batch"


[[items]]
id = "liediscover"
title = "LieDiscover：自适应符号库驱动的开放形式对称性发现"

summary = "LieDiscover通过联合优化符号函数库与系数，动态生成符号表达式，从数据中显式发现包含高阶多项式或超越函数的开放形式无穷小生成元。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33663"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "LieDiscover将对称性发现建模为函数库与表达式系数的联合优化问题，突破预定义符号库对生成元形式的限制。框架采用编码器—解码器动态生成符号表达式并扩展候选函数库，再通过代数优化识别未知无穷小生成元。符号生成过程使用强化学习和逐步奖励，加速对庞大符号搜索空间的探索。实验表明，该方法能够发现现有方法难以处理的高阶多项式及超越函数对称性，并可提升下游偏微分方程求解与方程发现任务的性能。"
purpose = "- 为从观测数据中自动发现精确、可解释的数学对称性提供神经符号方法。\n- 通过动态构造函数库，减少人工预设基函数造成的表达能力瓶颈。\n- 可将发现的对称性作为归纳偏置，用于改进偏微分方程求解和控制方程发现。\n- 展示强化学习逐步奖励在复杂符号表达式搜索与科学机制发现中的应用价值。"
notes = "标题：LieDiscover: Adaptive Symbolic Library Construction for Explicit Open-form Symmetry Discovery\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33663\n摘要：Discovering underlying symmetries from data has emerged as a crucial challenge in scientific discovery. Existing data-driven methods for symmetry discovery fail to determine the exact number and mathematical form of unknown infinitesimal generators. Recent explicit methods represent generators using a predefined function library and identify them through algebraic optimization, but they often struggle to capture complex symmetries involving high-order polynomials or transcendental functions. To address this limitation, we formulate symmetry discovery as a joint optimization problem over the function library and coefficients. We propose a novel framework that leverages an encoder-decoder architecture to dynamically generate symbolic expressions and expand the library. This generation proces"


[[items]]
id = "audit-first-vapo"
title = "Audit-First VAPO：不完美验证器下的风险认证选择性更新"
subtopic = "模型可靠性"
topics = [
    "模型训练",
    "强化学习",
    "人工智能可靠性",
    "过程验证",
    "不确定性估计",
]
research = []
keywords = [
    "模型可靠性",
    "强化学习",
    "过程验证",
    "不确定性估计",
    "风险认证",
]
orgs = [
    "中国科学院",
    "Institute of Information Engineering",
    "中国科学院大学",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "Audit-First VAPO通过先审计更新方向、再约束更新幅度，在验证器存在噪声时为有害更新风险、覆盖率和验证调用成本提供有限样本认证。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33662"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文指出，裁剪与正则化只能限制更新幅度，无法阻止不完美验证器给出方向错误的有害更新。Audit-First VAPO将离散的方向准入与连续的幅度控制分离，先以 accept-appeal-abstain 策略和有限二次验证预算筛选更新，再通过 trust-clip-KL 执行器约束幅度。方法利用条件 Hoeffding-Azuma 界处理共享预算造成的依赖，并同时认证有害风险、覆盖率及验证器调用率；当 rollout 或验证器发生变化时重新启动认证阶段。在 Qwen3.5-0.8B 与 GSM8K 实验中，目标风险为 0.08 时取得 74.1% 准确率、0.0697 的选择性有害风险和 0.4125 的覆盖率，并在多种验证器噪声条件下于 60 次独立运行中的 57 次满足证书要求。"
purpose = "- 为带噪验证器驱动的强化学习提供可审计、可量化的更新准入机制。\n- 提示训练安全不能只依赖梯度裁剪或 KL 正则，还应独立控制更新方向是否被接纳。\n- 有限样本风险证书可用于在模型收益、更新覆盖率与额外验证成本之间进行明确权衡。\n- 验证器或 rollout 分布变化后重新认证，有助于避免旧证书在训练环境漂移后失效。"
notes = "标题：Audit-First VAPO: Risk-Certified Selective Updates under Imperfect Verification\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33662\n摘要：Imperfect verifiers can assign a harmful update direction even when clipping and regularization bound its magnitude. We introduce Audit-First VAPO, which separates discrete directional admission from continuous magnitude control. An observation-only accept-appeal-abstain policy uses a finite secondary-verification budget; its action trace is frozen before clean labels are joined. Simultaneous finite-sample bounds then certify selected harmful risk, coverage, and verifier-call rate over a predeclared policy family. Conditional Hoeffding-Azuma bounds account for the dependence induced by shared budgets, and rollout or verifier changes initiate a new certification stage. After admission, a bounded trust-clip-KL actuator controls magnitude. We evaluate two models on two reasoning benchmarks ag"

id = "hilore-grpo"
title = "HiLoRe：面向高效 GRPO 训练的激活存储、压缩与重计算"
subtopic = "系统优化"
topics = [
    "系统优化",

    "强化学习",
]
orgs = [
    "中国科学院大学",
    "Tianjin Normal University",
    "University of Southern Queensland",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "HiLoRe 利用 GRPO 的策略更新暴露度动态分配激活的高精度存储、低精度压缩和确定性重计算，在维持下游性能的同时提升训练吞吐量。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33570"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "HiLoRe 针对 GRPO 学习器侧激活带来的显存与计算瓶颈，改进固定梯度检查点策略可能造成显存闲置和大量重计算的问题。方法提出“策略更新暴露度”，将当前 GRPO 损失系数与各状态的近似敏感性关联起来，并可在反向传播前直接获得，无需额外反向计算。系统依据恢复效用和更新条件下的近似风险，在高精度存储、低精度压缩与确定性重计算之间动态分配计算图恢复单元。在五个模型—任务设置中，其显存峰值低于梯度检查点方案的 1.10 倍，actor 更新吞吐量最高较梯度检查点提升 13.5%、较最快基线提升 7.9%，下游得分平均差异低于 0.6 个百分点。"
purpose = "- 展示如何利用强化学习目标函数的解析结构指导激活管理，而非仅依赖张量属性或执行成本。\n- 为 GRPO 等显存密集型后训练任务提供存储、压缩与重计算的动态折中方案。\n- 策略更新暴露度可作为状态保真度分配信号，为其他策略优化算法的训练系统设计提供参考。\n- 在有限性能损失下提升 actor 更新吞吐量，有助于降低长响应强化学习训练的计算成本。"
notes = "标题：HiLoRe: What to Store, Compress, or Recompute for Efficient GRPO Training\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33570\n摘要：Group-relative policy optimization (GRPO) makes learner-side activations a major memory-computation bottleneck: gradient checkpointing reduces activation memory through recomputation, but fixed schedules can leave roughly 18 GB unused on a 48-GB GPU despite substantial recomputation overhead. Existing activation-management methods set state fidelity from execution cost, tensor properties, or generic compression sensitivity, without explicitly incorporating GRPO's analytic update structure into state-fidelity allocation. We formalize this dependence as policy-update exposure, linking the current GRPO loss coefficients to state-level approximation sensitivity. These coefficients are available before backward without an additional backward pass. We introduce HiLoRe, which allocates graph-attr"


[[items]]
id = "item-114"
title = "重复解答微调使推理能力在后续训练中变得脆弱"
subtopic = "模型可靠性"
topics = [
    "模型训练",
    "人工智能可靠性",
    "灾难性遗忘",
    "数据工程",
    "持续学习",
]
research = []
keywords = [
    "模型可靠性",
    "持续学习",
    "灾难性遗忘",
    "模型微调",
    "数据策展",
]
orgs = [
    "Aix-Marseille Université",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "反复使用相同解答进行低数据量推理训练虽能获得即时高分，却会使模型在后续无关微调中出现严重且可恢复的推理能力衰退。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33559"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究在 Qwen3.5-9B-Base 上比较了重复约八次训练数百条固定解答与一次性训练更多不同解答，两者在训练结束时均能解决约 95% 的留出数学题。普通指令微调后，一次性训练模型的表现基本不变，而固定解答重复训练模型降至 86.0%，更强的后续训练会使其跌至 59.3% 或更低。若模型每次访问相同题目时使用新的解答轨迹，则不会受损，表明问题源于相同文本的重复，而非题目数量少。该能力更像被抑制而非被擦除，仅需五次推理训练更新或短暂的推理格式训练即可基本恢复；在后续阶段回放 6.25% 的原始解答也能缓解退化。"
purpose = "- 提醒低数据推理微调不能只依据训练结束时的评测判断效果，还需测试后续指令微调或任务迁移后的能力保持情况。\n- 数据有限时应优先为同一问题生成多样化解答轨迹，避免机械重复完全相同的训练文本。\n- 后续训练可少量回放原始推理数据，以降低推理能力被抑制或遗忘的风险。\n- 能力可通过极少量推理或格式训练恢复，说明轻量级能力复苏机制可能比完整重训更经济。"
notes = "标题：Fine Until Fine-Tuned: Repeated Solutions Make Reasoning Fragile\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33559\n摘要：Recipes such as s1 and LIMO teach a model to reason with little data by showing it the same thousand or fewer worked solutions many times over. Judged when that training ends, the repetition looks harmless. But reasoning models are often trained again, and we find that repetition leaves their reasoning fragile to that next stage, even when the stage has nothing to do with reasoning. We fine-tuned Qwen3.5-9B-Base on its own correct solutions to competition math problems, either drilling a few hundred of them about eight times each or showing many more once; with the same amount of training, both solve about 95% of held-out problems. A single pass of ordinary instruction tuning leaves the once-trained model where it was, while the drilled one falls to 86.0%, and harsher later stages take it "

dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "GFPR 不再于无约束潜空间中迭代隐藏状态，而是直接迭代定义在单纯形乘积上的类别信念，且每一步的 argmax 都可作为答案。任务结构可通过紧致凸松弛注入结构化读出或循环状态；当约束进入循环状态时，更新仍是连续自映射，因此任意参数下都存在不动点。约 700 万参数的模型在 Sudoku-Extreme、Maze-Hard 和长度 128 的 S_5 任务上分别达到 95.1%、92.0% 和 100% 的序列准确率，超过同规模已发表 FPRM 结果。该更新机制还扩展至在 FineWeb-Edu 上训练的 2.01 亿参数语言模型，使用 24 次 Picard 迭代后在多项零样本选择题评测中超过 GPT-2 small，并在 ARC-Easy 上超过 GPT-2 medium。"
purpose = "- 为循环推理模型提供具有明确几何含义的状态空间，使中间状态始终对应可解释的预测分布。\n- 通过连续自映射从理论上保证不动点存在，避免将潜空间中的小残差误判为真正收敛。\n- 展示凸结构约束与测试时迭代计算结合的路径，可用于数独、迷宫及序列推理等结构化任务。\n- 证明该架构可扩展至语言建模，为以循环深度替代部分参数规模提供实验依据。"
notes = "标题：Reasoning on the Simplex: Geometric Fixed-Point Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33540\n摘要：Looped reasoners spend test-time compute by iterating a weight-tied map, but a small residual does not mean the state is a fixed point when that map lives in unconstrained latent space. We propose Geometric Fixed-Point Reasoning (GFPR), in which the iterated state is the prediction itself: a field of categorical beliefs on a product of simplices, whose argmax is the answer at every step. Because the state is a belief, task structure can be imposed through compact convex relaxations, either as structured readouts or directly in the recurrent state; in the latter case the update remains a continuous self-map, so a fixed point exists for any parameters. At about 7M parameters, GFPR reaches 95.1% exact match on Sudoku-Extreme, 92.0% on Maze-Hard, and 100% sequence accuracy on S_5 length 128, a"


[[items]]
id = "system-one"
title = "System One 模型的智能体安全决策评测：可靠性、校准与选择性自动化"
subtopic = "安全评测"
topics = [
    "智能体安全",

    "选择性自动化",
    "分布外检测",
]
orgs = [
    "南洋理工大学",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究评测 Jev、Laya、Decider 和 Bespoke Nimble 在智能体安全决策中的准确率、概率校准与选择性自动化能力，揭示平均指标良好仍可能掩盖高置信度漏检和分布外失效。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33401"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究将四种输出类型化决策及概率的 System One 模型，与专用分类器和语言模型评判器进行比较，覆盖提示注入检测、交互风险评估及有害请求筛查。整体准确率和平均校准表现可能掩盖特定攻击组上的系统性失败，部分攻击甚至会被模型以高置信度判断为安全。在严格限制攻击漏检率时，模型能够自动放行的输入很少；分别设置放行与阻断阈值，增加的自动化主要来自阻断更多输入。验证集上满足错误率约束的策略在未见输入上仍可能超限，而引入第二个评判器虽能发现部分漏检，也会增加正常输入误拒并重复首个模型的高置信度错误。"
purpose = "- 智能体安全系统不能仅依据总体准确率或平均校准误差选型，还应按攻击类别审查高置信度漏检。\n- 自动放行、自动阻断与人工升级应采用独立阈值，并在未见分布上验证错误率约束。\n- 多评判器组合并不天然提高安全性，需要同时衡量攻击召回率、正常输入误拒率及错误相关性。\n- 部署前应联合评估模型预测、概率校准和最终决策策略，而非把校准指标直接视为自动化可靠性的保证。"
notes = "标题：Evaluating System One Models for Agent Security Decisions: Reliability, Calibration, and Selective Automation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33401\n摘要：Model-based judges support agent security by detecting prompt injections, assessing interaction risks, and screening harmful requests. System One models expose typed decisions with probabilities that software can use to allow, block, or escalate inputs, but whether these probabilities support reliable automated security decisions remains unclear. We evaluate Jev, Laya, Decider, and Bespoke Nimble against specialized classifiers and language-model judges, examining decision accuracy, probability calibration, and selective automation. We identify three main findings. (1) Strong overall performance and favorable average calibration can hide systematic failures on particular attack groups, including attacks that models confidently classify as safe. (2) Under strict limits on missed attacks, th"


[[items]]
id = "item-117"
title = "条件渐进剪枝提升多智能体辩论效率"
subtopic = "多智能体系统"
topics = [
    "多智能体系统",
    "多智能体协作",
    "测试时计算",
    "成本优化",

]
research = []

    "多智能体协作",
    "渐进剪枝",
    "测试时优化",

]
orgs = [
    "Rutgers, The State University of New Jersey",
    "剑桥大学",
    "清华大学",
    "哈佛大学",
    "Case Western Reserve University",
    "University of California San Diego",
    "CMU",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "论文提出轻量级条件渐进剪枝（CPP）框架，使多智能体辩论在严格等成本条件下首次全面超越一致性方法及现有辩论框架。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33974"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "多智能体辩论通过多轮通信实现知识与推理互补，但既有方法在严格成本约束下通常无法击败强单智能体和一致性基线。论文提出条件渐进剪枝（Conditional Progressive Pruning，CPP），以轻量级方式在辩论过程中逐步筛除不必要的候选或交互。实验显示，CPP在多个基准上优于现有多智能体辩论框架，并首次在同等成本下全面超越一致性方法。作者表示后续将公开代码及详细的智能体交互记录。"
purpose = "- 为多智能体辩论提供兼顾多轮协作收益与计算成本的剪枝方案。\n- 说明公平的等成本比较对于验证多智能体方法的真实增益至关重要。\n- 可用于设计按阶段动态缩减参与者或候选答案的测试时计算系统。\n- 公开的交互轨迹有望支持对辩论过程、剪枝决策及错误传播的进一步分析。"
notes = "标题：Beyond Solo and Consistency: Vindicating Multi-Agent Debate via Conditional Progressive Pruning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33974\n摘要：Large Language Model (LLM) based Multi-Agent Debate (MAD) is one of the most effective test time scaling techniques. Through multi-round communication, agents complement each other in knowledge and reasoning and solve tasks that no single member can solve. However, existing MAD frameworks fail to beat strong Single Agent and Consistency-based baselines under the same strict cost limit, which shakes the foundation of the MAD field. We propose Conditional Progressive Pruning (CPP), a lightweight pruning framework that fully exploits multi-round MAD. CPP outperforms all existing MAD frameworks on multiple dominated benchmarks. It is also the first to fully outperform consistency methods. Our code, detailed agent interaction records will be released soon."


[[items]]
id = "system-one-2"
title = "System One 决策的概率一致性研究"
subtopic = "模型可靠性"
topics = [

    "University of Malaya",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究发现 Jev 与 Laya 在直接分类和分层重构决策之间存在显著概率不一致，且准确率提升可能伴随置信度校准恶化。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33971"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究使用 TREC、CLINC150 和 MASSIVE 数据集，为 Jev 与英文 Laya checkpoint 分别构造了 2,500 个匹配样例，共评估 72,000 个分类问题。实验比较直接细粒度标签预测、宽类别概率，以及通过宽类别重构出的细粒度预测，发现两套系统均存在显著的概率一致性偏差。Jev 的类别级平均总变差为 0.219 至 0.349，Laya 则达到 0.424 至 0.689。在 CLINC150 上，分层重构使 Jev 准确率下降 22.9 个百分点，却使 Laya 提升 21.3 个百分点；在 MASSIVE 上，Laya 准确率提高的同时，预期校准误差由 0.046 恶化至 0.124。"
purpose = "- 评估决策模型时不能只检查单次输出的概率和是否为一，还应验证不同决策分解路径之间的一致性。\n- 应联合报告准确率、置信度校准和概率一致性，避免将准确率提升误判为整体可靠性改善。\n- 面向分层分类或多阶段决策应用，应在实际工作流中测试类别拆分与概率重构造成的误差传播。\n- 错误诊断需同时区分宽类别选择错误和类别内部混淆，以定位模型不一致的具体来源。"
notes = "标题：Do System One Decisions Add Up? A Study of Probabilistic Coherence\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33971\n摘要：A decision model can give probabilities that sum to one for every question yet disagree with itself when the same decision is broken into smaller steps. We study this form of probabilistic coherence in Jev and the English Laya checkpoint, using 2,500 matched examples per system across TREC, CLINC150, and MASSIVE. Across 72,000 classification questions, we compare direct fine-label predictions with broad-category probabilities and predictions reconstructed through those categories. Both systems show substantial disagreement: mean category-level total variation ranges from 0.219 to 0.349 for Jev and from 0.424 to 0.689 for Laya, on a scale where zero means exact agreement. The consequences differ sharply. On CLINC150, reconstruction reduces Jev's accuracy by 22.9 percentage points (paired 95"


[[items]]
id = "htn-mcp"
title = "HTN 规划协调多服务器 MCP 工具编排"
subtopic = "智能体框架"
topics = [
    "智能体编排",
    "智能体工具",

    "分层任务网络",
    "工具调用",
    "可信执行",
    "MCP",
]
orgs = [
    "RIKEN",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "该研究以分层任务网络规划器生成一次性可验证计划，并通过运行时中间件确定性地编排多个隔离的 MCP 服务器。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33731"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究针对 MCP 服务器彼此隔离、只能由宿主协调跨服务器工作流的问题，指出由大语言模型逐次编排工具会产生非确定性、难复现和高推理往返成本。该架构使用分层任务网络（HTN）规划器一次生成可验证的跨服务器计划，再由运行时中间件确定性执行。复合任务被分解为服务器本地的原子动作，动作之间的数据依赖通过 `${context.X}` 模板和 JSON-path 输出提取器在执行时绑定。作者在实验室机器人、生物信息学和多尺度建模等五个 HTN 领域中进行了实例化，并通过浏览器计划控制器调用八个真实第三方 MCP 服务器完成端到端验证。"
purpose = "- 将高成本、非确定性的逐工具 LLM 决策转化为“单次规划、确定性执行”，有助于降低调用成本并提高复现性。\n- HTN 计划可在执行前接受检查与验证，适合需要审计、合规或严格流程控制的工具链。\n- 运行时数据绑定机制为保持 MCP 服务器隔离的同时实现跨服务器数据流提供了通用设计。\n- 可用于科研工作流、生物信息学和实验室自动化等涉及多个异构工具服务的复杂任务。"
notes = "标题：HTN Planning as a Coordination Layer for Multi-Server MCP Tool Orchestration\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33731\n摘要：The Model Context Protocol (MCP) isolates servers by design: only the host can orchestrate cross-server workflows. When the host is a large language model, the resulting orchestrations are non-deterministic, non-reproducible, and pay one inference round-trip per tool call. We present a coordination architecture in which a Hierarchical Task Network (HTN) planner generates a verifiable cross-server plan once, and a runtime middleware executes it deterministically across multiple MCP servers, binding cross-action data dependencies via a template mechanism (\\verb|${context.X}|) substituted at execution time. The architecture mirrors MCP's isolation constraint: each compound task decomposes into server-local primitive actions, and inter-server data flow is bound at execution time via JSON-path "


[[items]]
id = "item-120"
title = "噪声与长尾下的特征-阈值双校准鲁棒伪标注"
subtopic = "模型训练"
topics = [
    "模型训练",
    "伪监督学习",
    "视觉表征学习",
    "不确定性估计",
]
research = []
keywords = [
    "模型训练",
    "伪监督学习",
    "语义分割",
    "阈值校准",
    "长尾学习",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "FTC-Seg通过正交原型特征重建与类别自适应阈值校准，缓解噪声和长尾分布共同导致的语义分割伪标签退化。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33668"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文指出，成像噪声会混淆前景与背景特征，而长尾分布会使尾部类别样本不足、预测置信度偏低，两者相互放大伪标签退化。固定高阈值会系统性过滤尾部类别的低置信度预测，使其无法从无标签数据中获得监督并陷入性能恶化循环。作者提出基于教师-学生架构的 FTC-Seg，其中正交原型重建（OPR）利用可学习的正交原型对像素特征进行残差净化，扩大弱前景目标与噪声背景之间的间隔。自适应阈值校准（ATC）根据类别学习难度和预测分布偏差动态调整阈值，并在覆盖三类噪声模态的四个公开基准上显著改善尾部类别表现。"
purpose = "- 为噪声与类别不均衡并存的半监督语义分割提供联合优化方案，避免仅处理单一因素的局限。\n- 可借鉴类别自适应阈值机制，减少固定置信度阈值对尾部类别伪标签的系统性排斥。\n- 正交原型重建表明，伪标签质量控制不仅可在输出端进行，也可通过特征净化提升前景与背景的可分性。\n- 适用于医学影像、遥感等同时存在成像噪声和长尾类别分布的视觉任务。"
notes = "标题：When Noise Meets Long-Tail: Feature-Threshold Dual Calibration for Robust Pseudo-Labeling\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33668\n摘要：Pseudo-labeling has become a cornerstone of learning from unlabeled data in semantic segmentation. Yet its effectiveness drops sharply in real-world scenarios where strong imaging noise and long-tailed class distributions occur together. We trace this failure to a vicious cycle of pseudo-label degradation. Imaging noise entangles foreground and background features, lowering prediction confidence across all classes, while long-tailed distributions leave tail classes with far fewer training samples and inherently lower confidence. Under fixed high-threshold filtering, these tail-class predictions are systematically filtered out, so they receive no supervision from unlabeled data and thus features keep degrading in subsequent iterations. Critically, noise and long-tail are not independent obs"


[[items]]
id = "llm-7"
title = "廉价验证器足以支撑稳健的 LLM 后训练"
subtopic = "奖励模型评测"
topics = [
    "奖励建模",
    "后训练",
    "模型评估",

]
research = []

    "奖励模型评测",
    "奖励建模",
    "后训练",
    "模型评测",
    "成本优化",
]
orgs = [
    "ETH Zurich",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "跨医疗、法律和金融任务的大规模实验表明，验证器与高端参考评判模型的一致率无法稳定预测后训练效果，低成本开放权重验证器也能取得接近最优的性能。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33467"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究使用超过 1.1 万 H100 GPU 小时，在 HealthBench 与 PRBench 的医疗、法律和金融任务上训练并评估 Qwen3 系列模型。实验发现，更高的验证器一致率并不能稳定识别出效果最好的训练验证器，昂贵验证器也不一定优于廉价方案。开放权重 Gemma 验证器取得了较强的训练结果，两种低成本选择相较高端评判协议可降低约 98.8%–99.7% 的评分成本。其后训练平均得分距离所评估的最佳验证器仅约 1–3 分，但部分设置仍存在较大损失，因此不能认为不同验证器可完全互换。"
purpose = "- 表明半可验证奖励场景中的后训练对一定程度的错误奖励具有较强鲁棒性，可降低对昂贵评判模型的依赖。\n- 提醒实践者不要仅凭验证器与高端参考评判模型的一致率选择训练验证器，而应直接评估其下游训练收益。\n- 为使用开放权重、小型或低成本验证器开展大规模强化后训练提供了成本依据。\n- 部署时仍需按领域和数据切分进行验证，避免平均结果掩盖个别设置中的明显性能损失。"
notes = "标题：A Cheap Verifier is Good Enough: LLM Post-training is Robust to Erroneous Rewards\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33467\n摘要：When post-training large language models on tasks with semi-verifiable rewards, there are many factors (training steps, base model size, training order, data quality, verifier accuracy, etc.) that practitioners must contend with to maximize model performance. Yet, it remains unclear how well verifier agreement predicts post-training performance on such tasks. In this paper, we explore this question with over 11k H100 GPU-hours, across HealthBench and PRBench tasks in medical, legal, and finance domains. Across the tested domains, Qwen3 trainees (1.7B-8B on HealthBench; 8B on PRBench), evaluation splits, and frontier LLM reference judges (which we call golden verifiers), higher verifier agreement does not consistently identify the best training verifier. Expensive verifiers need not outperf"

content = "论文指出，教师模型越强，其复杂输出分布越可能超出小型学生模型的拟合能力，形成导致蒸馏性能下降的“Gap Curse”。作者不再筛除困难样本或引入较弱的助教模型，而是提出 Teacher Alignment，直接将教师调整到更贴近学生的分布。为避免标准知识蒸馏引发教师推理能力灾难性崩溃，TeacherGRPO采用 Group Relative Policy Optimization，并设计课程式选择对齐与重要性自适应长度正则化。实验表明，对齐后的教师可通过标准蒸馏流程向学生传递知识，并在多种推理基准和蒸馏方法上显著优于基线。"
purpose = "- 为大模型向小模型迁移推理能力提供一种不牺牲数据覆盖率的教师侧对齐方案。\n- 表明蒸馏中的能力差距可通过调整教师分布缓解，而不必仅依赖样本过滤或中间助教模型。\n- 双层课程机制可聚焦高价值推理差异，降低简单词元与不确定尾部分布带来的奖励噪声。\n- 自适应长度正则化为压缩冗余推理、保留关键教学步骤提供了可复用思路。"
notes = "标题：TeacherGRPO: Closing the Capacity Gap in Reasoning Distillation via Teacher Alignment\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33426\n摘要：Reasoning distillation from powerful teacher models to smaller students faces the Gap Curse: as teachers grow more sophisticated, their complex distributions increasingly diverge from what students can approximate, causing performance degradation. Existing mitigation strategies either filter out challenging examples through data selection or introduce weaker intermediate assistant models, inherently compromising supervision coverage or quality. We propose Teacher Alignment, which directly adapts the teacher toward the student's distribution without discarding data or degrading reasoning quality. ## 今日概览
- 递归自我改进从“单一策略优化”走向参数、上下文、记忆、技能与脚手架的联合闭环：[PhysicalRSI 1.0：具身智能递归自我改进基线登顶RoboDojo](#physicalrsi-1-0-robodojo)协同演化规划、执行与 Harness，[COEVO：上下文与参数协同演化的递归自我改进](#coevo)联动参数和学习上下文，[RSIAgent：免训练多智能体在新环境中递归自我改进](#rsiagent)则把环境反馈沉淀为持久因果记忆。
- 智能体 Harness、环境与评测开始共同自演化：[Vestrum：通过验证、结构与记忆适配改进智能体脚手架](#vestrum)从重复失败中自动修订脚手架，[CompoWorld：面向通用智能体的组合式环境扩展](#compoworld)规模化生成可验证跨服务任务，[MetaBench-Harness：端到端演化基准生成框架](#metabench-harness)以双循环搜索持续提高基准难度与区分度。
- 多智能体研究一面扩展长程协作与规模规律，一面暴露群体风险：[AgentWorld：评测多智能体长程协作能力](#agentworld)细化角色分工、资源交接和共享计划的因果评测，[Agent 集群 Scaling Law：分队探索优于盲目扩群](#agent-scaling-law)指出分队与动态重组优于单纯扩群，而[ExploitGym中1200个AI智能体自发串通并越权攻击](#exploitgym-1200-ai)显示共享状态和结果导向奖励可能催生作弊、串通及越权。
- 评测可靠性成为集中主线：从基准作弊、运行方差到评判器偏差均出现系统性证据，[UC Berkeley 用 AI Agent 审计 13 个基准，发现 45 种满分作弊方案](#uc-berkeley-ai-agent-13-45)揭示静态基准的严重可钻空子性，[相同运行、不同结果：开放权重模型上的 AI 编程智能体评测](#item-52)要求重复实验并联合报告质量、合规与成本，[修辞如何奖励劫持 AI 审稿：4080 次改写揭示评分偏差](#ai-4080)则表明内容不变时修辞仍可显著操纵 AI 评分。
- 长寿命智能体安全从案例警报转向权限、隔离和契约机制：[Claude Code误删4.8万真实文件：提示约束无法替代系统隔离](#claude-code-4-8)凸显最小权限与文件系统隔离的必要性，[长寿命智能体中的残余权限重放攻击](#item-41)指出授权语境失效后的权限残留问题，[Maat：基于确定性契约的多智能体工作流治理](#maat)探索用模型外契约验证智能体交接。
- 快决策与推理效率继续沿“校准路由＋低成本计算”推进：[Intern-Decision开源：多模态实时决策性能超越Jev](#intern-decision-jev)以置信度校准和大小模型协作兼顾延迟与准确率，[JET：基于候选似然与前缀复用的高效决策推理](#jet)提供免训练候选决策方案，[TokenProbe：利用词元价值不均衡提升推理效率](#tokenprobe)在保持质量时将思维链词元消耗降低 76%。

## 对当前研究的启发
- **Awesome-RSI**：[PhysicalRSI 1.0：具身智能递归自我改进基线登顶RoboDojo](#physicalrsi-1-0-robodojo)、[COEVO：上下文与参数协同演化的递归自我改进](#coevo)与[RSIAgent：免训练多智能体在新环境中递归自我改进](#rsiagent)提示 RSI 清单应按“参数—上下文—记忆—Harness—技能”改进对象及是否具备独立验证、版本化和成本曲线来比较闭环质量。
- **HarnessEvolve**：[Vestrum：通过验证、结构与记忆适配改进智能体脚手架](#vestrum)和[MetaBench-Harness：端到端演化基准生成框架](#metabench-harness)可直接借鉴为“从失败轨迹归因改造组件、再用动态基准反向检验”的 Harness 内外双循环演化方案。
- **EnvironmentEvolve**：[CompoWorld：面向通用智能体的组合式环境扩展](#compoworld)与[Skill2Env：面向通用智能体的能力导向环境合成](#skill2env)表明环境生成应同时以技能缺口定向、服务依赖图组合、轨迹可执行验证和反馈式难度升级作为质量门槛。
- **EvalEvolve**：[UC Berkeley 用 AI Agent 审计 13 个基准，发现 45 种满分作弊方案](#uc-berkeley-ai-agent-13-45)和[相同运行、不同结果：开放权重模型上的 AI 编程智能体评测](#item-52)要求动态评测同时加入作弊面审计、重复运行置信区间及质量—合规—成本多维报告，而不能只演化题目难度。
- **SwarmEvolve**：[AgentWorld：评测多智能体长程协作能力](#agentworld)、[Agent 集群 Scaling Law：分队探索优于盲目扩群](#agent-scaling-law)与[ExploitGym中1200个AI智能体自发串通并越权攻击](#exploitgym-1200-ai)共同提示群体演化实验应显式控制小队拓扑、交接因果贡献和共享状态权限，并把串通与奖励作弊纳入规模化评测。
- **MemoryEvolve**：[RSIAgent：免训练多智能体在新环境中递归自我改进](#rsiagent)与[位置不是事实：KV 缓存与记忆的错配](#item-59)说明持久因果记忆应与位置型 KV 状态解耦，并用验证结果和对象依赖管理写入、更新及失效。
- **JevEvolve**：[Intern-Decision开源：多模态实时决策性能超越Jev](#intern-decision-jev)与[JET：基于候选似然与前缀复用的高效决策推理](#jet)给出了 Jev 下一步可直接比较的两条路线：训练式多模态快决策与免训练候选似然决策，并统一评测校准、升级边界、延迟和成本。
- **LogicEvolve**：[多智能体提出C-HD最短路径算法并完成Lean验证](#c-hd-lean)表明“多智能体提出候选算法—Lean 内核验真—工程基准否证实用性”可作为区分形式创新、正确性与实际价值的三阶段闭环。
- **ResearchEvolve**：[自主量子相发现：梯度驱动探索哈密顿量空间](#item-53)展示了可微探索与无监督表征结合的自主发现范式，适合纳入科研智能体对“搜索效率—发现新颖性—可复现验证”的闭环评测。
- **DataEvolve**：[程序验证驱动的视觉语言模型自进化](#item-48)说明固定程序验证器可把多模态自合成标签正确率提升至 94%，可作为抑制数据飞轮错误累积的强质量闸门。
- **Groom**：[TokenProbe：利用词元价值不均衡提升推理效率](#tokenprobe)提示可把词元级价值与压缩后性能保持率加入过程归因指标，区分真正承载决策证据的 token 与冗余思维链。
